Door prose is N02–N05's work at pickup. Any sentence here that drafts a door's actual wording is scope creep — reject it at review.\n\n## Problem\n\nThe shaping half of the pack is spread across twelve skills (2,599 lines at the 2026-06-12 baseline) plus two execution-side survivors, with overlapping When-to-use sections that force a routing guess on every session — `using-this-pack` papers over the ambiguity rather than removing it. Three skills sit over the pack's own 300-line cap (`delivery-shape` 381, `plan-review` 369, `roadmap-shape` 303), so the pack fails its own authoring standard.\n\nFour door builds (N02–N05) and a utility pass (N06) are queued to consolidate this, but they are blocked on decisions no single node can safely make alone: which sections of eight legacy skills fold into which door, what is deliberately deleted, what the surviving file set is, what everything is named, and where every `[GATE]` lands. Decided per-door, those four judgement calls drift apart, and a dropped gate surfaces only at the benchmark (N07) or in a live shaping run — not at review. The risk is not hypothetical: the source library already contains one silent gate loss (delivery-shape — see Context), which this doc converts from an undetected drift into a tracked decision.\n\nAffected parties: the door authors (N02–N06 wait on this doc for their task breakdowns), the N08 reference sweep (needs one deletion list), drain-cycle workers and Anton driving interactively (every session pays the routing and context cost), and initiative C (its supervisor prompts will cite door verbs literally).\n\nCurrent behaviour: no absorption map, no pinned measured set, no naming decision, no gate inventory. Desired behaviour: one accepted IA document that fixes all four, so the door builds become mechanical compression jobs against a stable spec.\n\n## Context\n\n- **Confirmed intent (not re-litigated here).** `docs/ideas/lifecycle-expansion.md:40` records the owner-confirmed pruning: roadmap-shape, backlog-manage, app-calibrate deleted; idea-triage folds into `shape:idea`; spikes fold into `shape:design`; plan-review survives as the design/delivery exit gate; render-html survives as a utility. This doc inherits those calls and decides the *section-level* mechanics.\n- **Namespace inherited from A/N04.** ADR 0004 reserves both halves of the verb namespace (`shape:*` / `exec:*`) in one table and explicitly leaves the shaping-half consolidation to this initiative. This doc consumes that table; it does not reopen it. The shaping **leaf** names decided here get their own decision record: **ADR 0005, filed at acceptance**, mirroring the ADR 0004 pattern.\n- **Prior IA superseded.** `docs/designs/shaping-pipeline.md` (2026-06-03, status Proposed, never accepted) is the previous shaping IA. Its surviving decisions already live in delivery-shape (five-section ticket body, size gate); its `execution-breakdown` construct was deleted in `2c03385`. This doc supersedes it; N08 need not rewrite it — superseded docs are historical record, like CHANGELOG.\n- **Anatomy already reformed.** `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (revised in `e0cd32f`, 2026-06-12) already specifies the two-field frontmatter and the eleven-section body. The frontmatter-cut spec below binds the doors to it and lists which survivors still carry legacy fields.\n- **Baseline drift since the issues were written — delivery-shape, measured against git.** The cycle issues carry 2026-06-10 numbers. Re-measured 2026-06-12 (`wc -l skills/*/SKILL.md`): the anatomy-prune (`cfe42f6`) cut design-doc 243→174 and initiative-shape to 175 (under cap, six-field shape intact). delivery-shape was **196 lines at `cfe42f6`** and was then deliberately grown to **381** (`ac931a7` — walking-skeleton conditionality; `372f09f`/`fc9b497` — +293/−108 overall). The added content is **normative contract, not padding**: the `Done when:` discipline, the size check, dependency ordering, and the model-routing rubric that `bin/check-plan-framing` enforces. N02's premise (\"no fold needed, already under cap at 266\") is doubly stale — see Q1.\n- **A silent gate loss already happened in delivery-shape.** At `45b49b9` the file carried **seven** `[GATE]` markers (2 committed-input, 3 map-to-KRs, 4 Rule A1 design-first, 6 five-section body, 7c acceptance criterion, 7d size check, 9 verify-plan). The current file carries **one** marked gate (step 2). The substance of the other six survives as unmarked steps/prose (steps 3, 4, 6, 9 and the step-7 task rules), but a fitness check that counts gate markers would bless their deletion. See Q4 and the inventory's delivery-shape ledger.\n- **Initiative A is concurrently adding execution skills** to `skills/` (build, debugging, exec-pickup, execution-review, pr-finishing, simplify), so `wc -l skills/*/SKILL.md` drifts for reasons outside this initiative. The measured set below pins exactly which files KR1 counts.\n- Prior artefacts loaded: `docs/design-docs/execution-workflow/design-doc.md`, ADR 0001–0004, `docs/ideas/lifecycle-expansion.md`, `docs/skill-anatomy.md`, `install.sh` (wrapper generation :20–38, symlink prune :77–84, symlink creation :86–93, hardcoded help text :161–173), `bin/eval-triggers`, `.claude/commands/` inventory, the N01–N06 plan sources under `examples/delivery-plans/shaping-four-front-doors/`.\n\n## Constraints\n\n**Functional.**\n\n- Every gate in the eight folded skills has a destination section or a written deletion rationale (the issue's mandatory Done condition).\n- The six-field initiative shape emitted by today's initiative-shape is byte-stable across the fold — `shape:delivery`'s gate 2 consumes it verbatim.\n- `bin/walk-delivery-plan` and `bin/check-plan-framing` keep exiting 0 on existing example plans after the delivery re-house.\n- `bin/eval-triggers` keeps running: its `CANDIDATE_SKILLS` array hardcodes `delivery-shape` and `initiative-shape` (`bin/eval-triggers:32–35`) and reads `eval/delivery-shape-triggers.md`. N02 retargets the delivery entry; N04 retargets the initiative entry. The eval files themselves are renamed or re-pointed in the same change.\n- delivery-shape's **protected content** survives the re-house and compression: the step-7 contract rules (`Done when:`, size check, dependency ordering, model routing) and the six de-marked gates' substance. Extraction to the existing `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` is the sanctioned overflow route; deletion is not.\n- Folded source directories and their wrappers are deleted in the same change as the door that absorbs them (revert restores both sides atomically).\n- No stack-prescriptive content enters door prose (repo rule); the doors stay stack-agnostic.\n\n**Non-functional.**\n\n| NFR | Target | Fitness function |\n|---|---|---|\n| Per-skill cap | Each door and each survivor ≤300 lines; 350 is the hard cap and 300–350 ships only flagged | `wc -l` on the file in each node's Done-when |\n| Total footprint | Measured set ≤2,200 lines | the N06 footprint check: `wc -l` over the seven manifest files below |\n| Gate preservation | 19/19 fold-source gates accounted for (15 mapped, 4 deleted with rationale) **plus** delivery-shape's 7-gate ledger (Q4); zero silent losses | grep of `Gate:` headers per door diffed against the Gate inventory at N03–N05 Done-when; N08 sweeps for stragglers. Known false positive: `using-this-pack:60` explains the `[GATE]` convention in prose — N06's rewrite rephrases it or the sweep whitelists it |\n| Benchmark stability | plan-review benchmark ≥90% after reform (kill condition: <80% after two repair attempts) | N07 rerun of `skills/plan-review/evals/evals.json` (kept byte-identical by N06) |\n| Zero legacy citations | grep for the seven deleted/renamed skill names returns empty over the **live surfaces**: README, install.sh (including the help text), hooks, `using-this-pack`, `.claude/commands/`, `bin/`, `eval/`, `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md`. Exempt as historical record: CHANGELOG, `examples/delivery-plans/`, `docs/plan-reviews/`, superseded docs | N08's sweep task |\n| Wrapper collision | No two commands share a leaf name at the slash surface | diff over `.claude/commands/` after authoring (same fitness as A/N04's NFR) |\n\n## Alternatives considered\n\nThe fold/delete/survive calls are inherited (Context); the open decision this doc owns is the **naming schema** — directory names, wrapper names, and how they bind to the `shape:*` verbs.\n\n**Alt 1 — bare phase-leaf directories (`skills/idea/`, `skills/project/`, `skills/design/`, `skills/delivery/`), wrapper leaf = directory leaf.**\n*Description.* Directory, frontmatter `name`, and wrapper file all use the phase leaf; `install.sh` derives the installed symlink `shape-<leaf>` and the wrapper publishes `/shape:<leaf>`. Exactly the rule the pack already applies (`delivery.md` → `/shape:delivery`).\n*Blast radius if wrong.* Low. Leafs are generic out of context (\"design\") but never appear out of context — the installed surface always carries the `shape-`/`shape:` prefix.\n*Reversal cost.* Low — `install.sh` already prunes stale symlinks on re-install; a rename is a directory move plus one wrapper edit.\n\n**Alt 2 — suffixed directories (`skills/idea-shape/`, …).**\n*Description.* Keep a descriptive suffix on every directory.\n*Blast radius if wrong.* Installed names stutter (`shape-idea-shape`); wrapper leaf no longer equals directory leaf, so the naming rule needs a lookup table instead of a convention.\n*Reversal cost.* Same mechanics as Alt 1 but with a worse steady state; rejected on stutter and rule complexity.\n\n**Alt 3 — keep current directories, retarget wrappers only.**\n*Description.* `/shape:idea` points at `skills/idea-triage/`, etc.; no directory moves.\n*Blast radius if wrong.* Permanent drift between verb and directory; the KR's cleanup grep (\"zero legacy citations\") can never pass because the legacy names *are* the layout; the router has to explain the mismatch forever.\n*Reversal cost.* High in practice — the drift compounds with every doc that cites a path.\n\n**Alt 4 — do nothing (no consolidation).**\n*Description.* Keep the twelve shaping skills.\n*Blast radius if wrong.* KR1 fails by definition; the routing ambiguity and over-cap skills persist.\n*Reversal cost.* n/a — this is the failure mode the initiative exists to remove.\n\n## Recommended approach\n\n**Adopt Alt 1.** The four artefacts below are the spec the door nodes build against.\n\n### 1. Naming schema (consumes ADR 0004's table; recorded in ADR 0005 at acceptance)\n\n| Verb | Directory | Frontmatter `name` | Wrapper (repo) | Slash command | Installed symlink |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `shape:idea` | `skills/idea/` | `idea` | `.claude/commands/idea.md` | `/shape:idea` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-idea` |\n| `shape:project` | `skills/project/` | `project` | `.claude/commands/project.md` | `/shape:project` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-project` |\n| `shape:design` | `skills/design/` | `design` | `.claude/commands/design.md` | `/shape:design` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-design` |\n| `shape:delivery` | `skills/delivery/` | `delivery` | `.claude/commands/delivery.md` (retargeted) | `/shape:delivery` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-delivery` |\n| `shape:plan-review` | `skills/plan-review/` (unchanged) | `plan-review` | `.claude/commands/plan-review.md` (unchanged) | `/shape:plan-review` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-plan-review` |\n| `shape:render-html` | `skills/render-html/` (unchanged) | `render-html` | `.claude/commands/render-html.md` (unchanged) | `/shape:render-html` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-render-html` |\n| — (router, model-invoked) | `skills/using-this-pack/` (unchanged) | `using-this-pack` | none (status quo) | — | `~/.claude/skills/shape-using-this-pack` |\n\nRules the table encodes: wrapper leaf = directory leaf = frontmatter `name`; `install.sh` derives the symlink (`shape-<leaf>`) and prunes stale links on re-install (verified `install.sh:77–84`). **Not derived — owned by hand:** the help text at `install.sh:161–173` hardcodes the command list and currently prints eight legacy verbs; each door node updates its own lines there (N02 proves the seam), and N08's grep catches stragglers.\n\n**Wrapper disposition.** Deleted with their skills: `idea-triage.md`, `initiative.md`, `roadmap.md`, `backlog-manage.md`, `design-doc.md`, `backend-spike.md`, `product-spike.md`. Created: `idea.md`, `project.md`, `design.md`. Retargeted in place: `delivery.md`. Out of this initiative's scope: `exec/*`, `stop-the-line.md`, `ta[REDACTED_SK].md`, `pr-prepare.md`, `verify-implementation.md` (execution half — initiative A owns their fate; N08 only sweeps citations).\n\n**Plugin manifest.** `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`'s description currently names deleted capabilities (\"idea triage, roadmap shaping, …\"). N06 rewrites it to the four-door vocabulary alongside the router rewrite.\n\n### 2. Survivor manifest and the measured set\n\nThe shaping library after consolidation is exactly seven files — four doors plus three utilities. **This is the measured set for the KR1 ≤2,200 check**, pinned so initiative A's concurrent additions to `skills/` cannot drift the count. The set counts `SKILL.md` lines only; bundled skill-local reference files (e.g. `skills/render-html/references/`) are explicitly outside it:\n\n```\nwc -l skills/idea/SKILL.md skills/project/SKILL.md skills/design/SKILL.md \\\n      skills/delivery/SKILL.md skills/plan-review/SKILL.md \\\n      skills/render-html/SKILL.md skills/using-this-pack/SKILL.md\n```\n\n| Survivor | Built from (2026-06-12 lines) | Size plan |\n|---|---|---|\n| `shape:idea` | idea-triage (220) + app-calibrate fragments (155) | ≤300 (1.25:1 fold) |\n| `shape:project` | initiative-shape (175) + roadmap/backlog fragments | ≤300 (mostly 1:1; fragments are clauses, not sections) |\n| `shape:design` | design-doc (174) + backend-spike (205) + product-spike (189) = 568 | ≤300 target, 350 hard — the kill-condition watch node (N05) |\n| `shape:delivery` | delivery-shape (381) | ≤300 via contract-aware compression with the overflow route to `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md`; protected content named in Constraints (Q1) |\n| `plan-review` | plan-review (369) | ≤300 — density reform only, gates and evals.json untouched (N06) |\n| `render-html` | render-html (131) | unchanged bar frontmatter cut; its bundled `references/` dir travels with it |\n| `using-this-pack` | using-this-pack (119) | rewritten router, **≤150** (N06) |\n\nWorst-case projection with every capped file at exactly 300 and the router at its 150 bound: 300×5 + 131 + 150 = **1,781**; modelling `shape:design` at its flagged 350 hard cap instead: **1,831 ≤ 2,200** (≥369 headroom, ≈17%). Excluded from the measured set: every `exec`-half skill, including the two baseline skills transferred to initiative A (`pr-prepare`, `verify-implementation`).\n\n### 3. Frontmatter-cut spec\n\nPer `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (already revised): exactly two fields survive — `name`, `description` (what the skill does, then \"Use when …\", then trigger phrases). The eight legacy fields die: `pack`, `lifecycle_stage`, `principles_implemented`, `length_target`, `author`, `predecessor`, `kept_from_predecessor`, `changed_from_predecessor`. Taxonomy they carried lives in README tables and git history.\n\nState of play: initiative-shape, design-doc, delivery-shape are already cut. Still carrying legacy fields among survivors: **plan-review (10 fields), render-html (10), using-this-pack (6)** — N06 applies the cut. The folded sources' frontmatter dies with their files.\n\n### 4. Absorption map — all 14 baseline skills\n\nDisposition vocabulary: **fold** (content moves into a door section), **absorb** (a clause or fragment moves; the section dies), **delete** (dies with written rationale), **survive** (file continues, possibly re-housed), **transfer** (leaves the shaping half for initiative A's execution half).\n\nA door's body follows the eleven-section anatomy (Title → Purpose → When to use → Do not use when → Inputs → Outputs → Workflow → Artefact template → Red flags → Exit criteria → Related). Two standing rules applied throughout, recorded once instead of per-row:\n\n- **\"Common rationalisations\" sections** (idea-triage, app-calibrate, roadmap-shape, backlog-manage, backend-spike, product-spike, plan-review, render-html) have no slot in the anatomy. Rule: load-bearing rows merge into the door's **Red flags**; the rest is explanatory redundancy and is deleted. plan-review and render-html keep theirs until their own density passes apply the same rule.\n- **\"References\" sections** become the door's **Related** section, citing only references the door actually uses.\n\n**idea-triage (220) → fold into `shape:idea`** — the door's core.\n\n| Source section | Disposition |\n|---|---|\n| Purpose / When to use / When not to use | fold → door Purpose / When to use / Do not use when; When-to-use gains one branch clause absorbed from app-calibrate (below) |\n| Inputs / Outputs | fold → door Inputs / Outputs |\n| Workflow (7 steps; gates at steps 2, 5, 7) | fold → door Workflow; all three gates carried (inventory G1–G3) |\n| Artefact template (Raw intake / Problem restatement / Evidence / ICE score / Routing / Notes) | fold → door Artefact template; the Routing entry adds the parked-idea-bank entry format absorbed from backlog-manage |\n| Common rationalisations / Red flags / Verification | per standing rules → Red flags / Exit criteria |\n| References (ice-scoring, confidence-meter, app-context-schema) | fold → Related; add kano-classification.md (used by the ICE/Kano step; today cited only by roadmap-shape) |\n\n**app-calibrate (155) → absorb fragments into `shape:idea`; delete the rest.**\n\n| Source section | Disposition |\n|---|---|\n| Purpose / When to use / When not to use | delete — the trigger collapses to one When-to-use branch clause in the door: *idea targets a measurable improvement and `docs/app-context.md` is missing or stale (>90 days) → ground the baseline first* |\n| Workflow steps 1–3, 5 (gather, probe, write) | delete — the procedure compresses to one door workflow step: populate/refresh `docs/app-context.md` per `references/app-context-schema.md` |\n| Workflow step 4 `[GATE]` minimum validity | **absorb** → clause on the door's scoring gate (inventory G4) |\n| Artefact template | delete — duplicate of `references/app-context-schema.md`, which survives and is the single schema of record |\n| Extension: MCP data sources | delete — optional enrichment never exercised; the schema reference keeps the field list |\n| Rationalisations / Red flags / Verification / References | per standing rules; `app-context-schema.md` cited from the door's Related |\n\n**initiative-shape (175) → fold-in-full into `shape:project`** — every section survives 1:1 (it is already anatomy-conformant and under cap). The six-field initiative description template is **byte-stable**: `shape:delivery` gate 2 consumes it verbatim, and N04's Done-when re-runs that gate on an existing initiative description. Gates 2, 7, 8 carried (inventory G5–G7). N04 also retargets the `initiative-shape` entry in `bin/eval-triggers`.\n\n**roadmap-shape (303) → delete.** Rationale: portfolio-planning ceremony the single-user reality never exercised — ideas route individually through `shape:idea` and become initiatives through `shape:project`; there is no planning-cycle ritual to host the Now/Next/Later board, capacity allocation, or theme distribution. Roadmap *review* still has a home: plan-review's When-to-use already covers roadmaps.\n\n| Source section | Disposition |\n|---|---|\n| Purpose / When / Inputs / Outputs / Workflow (12 steps) | delete; gates at steps 3, 6, 12 individually dispositioned in the inventory (G8–G10) |\n| Roadmap template (Now/Next/Later, capacity allocation, theme distribution, validation slots) | delete — `references/portfolio-themes.md` is thereby orphaned → N08 deletion list |\n| Shape review | delete — superseded by the surviving plan-review utility |\n| Rationalisations / Red flags / Verification / References | delete; ice-scoring / confidence-meter / kano-classification / task-sizing all remain cited by survivors |\n\n**backlog-manage (178) → delete.** Rationale: idea-bank curation ceremony (review sessions, promote/kill passes, evidence feedback) never exercised as a session of its own. What must survive, survives elsewhere: parking an idea is `shape:idea`'s routing output; promoting a parked idea is re-running `shape:idea` on it; KTLO logging convention already lives in `docs/ktlo.md`.\n\n| Source section | Disposition |\n|---|---|\n| Purpose / When / Inputs / Outputs | delete (rationale above) |\n| Workflow step 3 `[GATE]` session intent | delete (inventory G11) |\n| Workflow step 5 `[GATE]` confirm writes | **absorb** → clause on `shape:idea`'s routing gate (inventory G12) |\n| Remaining workflow steps | delete |\n| Artefact template (bank entry / ledger) | absorb the parked-entry essentials into `shape:idea`'s Artefact template (Routing entry); the curation ledger dies |\n| Rationalisations / Red flags / Verification / References | per standing rules; no orphaned references |\n\n**design-doc (174) → fold-in-full into `shape:design`** as the doc branch. The door gains a typed branch at its trigger gate: *problem understood + trigger fires → doc branch; the open question is itself the unknown → spike branch (technical or product variant)*. design-doc's \"Do not use when: problem not understood → spike first\" therefore becomes internal routing instead of a cross-skill referral. Gates 1, 3, 8 carried (inventory G13–G15). Artefact template survives as the doc-branch template; Related keeps `nfr-categories.md`.\n\n**backend-spike (205) → fold into `shape:design`** as the technical-spike variant.\n\n| Source section | Disposition |\n|---|---|\n| Purpose / When / When-not | fold → the door's When-to-use spike-branch triggers |\n| Workflow (gates at steps 1, 4, 6) | fold → spike-branch steps; gates carried (inventory G16–G18); steps shared with the product variant where identical (write-the-question, scope/timebox) |\n| Recommendation template (Question / Failure example / Options considered / Scope / Recommended approach / Confidence and semantics impact / Follow-up) | fold → merged **spike record** template, technical-variant fields |\n| Rationalisations / Red flags / Verification / References | per standing rules; confidence-meter stays cited |\n\n**product-spike (189) → fold into `shape:design`** as the product-spike variant. Same pattern: When-to-use triggers fold into the spike branch; the finding template (Question / Approach / Observations / Finding / Recommendation / Prototype artefact) merges into the spike record template as the product-variant fields; gate at step 5 merges into the shared spike exit gate (inventory G19). The throwaway-artefact discipline (prototype is disposable, finding is the deliverable) survives as spike-branch prose.\n\n**delivery-shape (381) → survive, re-housed as `shape:delivery`** (N02, the walking skeleton). Every section folds 1:1 into `skills/delivery/`; the emitted plan format and `bin/` gate compatibility are asserted by N02's Done-when, and the `bin/eval-triggers` entry is retargeted. Reaching ≤300 is **not a density pass** — the growth from 196 (`cfe42f6`) to 381 is recent, deliberate contract content. The sanctioned route is **contract-aware compression**: protected content (Constraints) stays in the skill or moves intact to `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` with a citation; nothing protected is deleted. Gate handling per Q4: the six de-marked gates are re-marked (or their demotion is owner-recorded) before any compression starts, so the gate fitness check has a true baseline.\n\n**plan-review (369) → survive** as a utility. N06 compresses to ≤300 as pure density reform: all six `[GATE]` steps verbatim, `evals/evals.json` byte-identical, tier-selection and B1–B8 axes unchanged. Frontmatter cut applies.\n\n**render-html (131) → survive** as a utility, unchanged except the frontmatter cut. Its references are **bundled skill-local files** (`skills/render-html/references/html-patterns.md`, `html-skeleton.md`) cited via `<skill-base>/references/…` — they exist, travel with the directory, and stay outside the measured set.\n\n**using-this-pack (119) → survive** as the router, rewritten by N06 to route exactly the four doors plus utilities (and to point execution work at `exec:pickup`), bounded at ≤150 lines. Frontmatter cut applies (currently 6 fields). No gates; meta-pack anatomy exemptions apply. Line 60's prose explanation of the `[GATE]` convention is rephrased or whitelisted so N08's straggler grep stays clean.\n\n**pr-prepare (163) and verify-implementation (123) → transfer** to the execution half. ADR 0004 reserves their successors (`exec:finish`, `exec:verify`); initiative A owns the skills, their gates (2 + 2), and their wrappers. They leave the shaping measured set. N08 sweeps shaping-side citations only.\n\n**Reference disposition** (consequence of the map): survive — app-context-schema, confidence-meter, ice-scoring, kano-classification, initiative-types, kr-quality-templates, task-sizing, nfr-categories, plus render-html's bundled pair. Orphaned → N08 deletion list — portfolio-themes (only roadmap-shape cites it), dora-metrics (cited by no skill; README/CHANGELOG only).\n\n### 5. Gate inventory — every gate in the eight folded skills\n\n19 gates at the 2026-06-12 baseline (line numbers from that baseline): 15 with confirmed destinations, 4 deleted with rationale. N03–N05's Done-whens check door gate headers against this table, not reviewer memory.\n\n| # | Source gate | Destination / deletion rationale |\n|---|---|---|\n| G1 | idea-triage:83 — step 2 \"Diagnose: problem or solution?\" | `shape:idea` Workflow — early gate, carried verbatim |\n| G2 | idea-triage:105 — step 5 \"Does the problem statement hold?\" | `shape:idea` Workflow — carried verbatim |\n| G3 | idea-triage:118 — step 7 \"Route the idea.\" | `shape:idea` Workflow — final routing gate, carried; gains two absorbed clauses (G4 grounding, G12 confirm-writes) |\n| G4 | app-calibrate:79 — step 4 \"Minimum validity check.\" | **absorbed** as a clause on `shape:idea`'s scoring/evidence gate: an ICE Impact score that cites no `app-context.md` baseline is marked ungrounded |\n| G5 | initiative-shape:43 — gate 2 \"problem or solution?\" | `shape:project` Workflow — carried verbatim |\n| G6 | initiative-shape:94 — gate 7 \"user confirmation\" | `shape:project` Workflow — carried verbatim (also the surviving home of tracker-write confirmation) |\n| G7 | initiative-shape:100 — gate 8 \"verification rubric — 11 cross-cutting rules\" | `shape:project` Workflow — carried verbatim; rubric content untouched |\n| G8 | roadmap-shape:108 — step 3 \"Strip any solution framing.\" | **deleted** — duplicate: the identical check survives twice, as G1 (`shape:idea`) and G5 (`shape:project`); every path to a roadmap-shaped artefact now passes one of them |\n| G9 | roadmap-shape:129 — step 6 \"Confidence gate (Rule B6).\" | **deleted** — superseded by G3: `shape:idea`'s routing gate already forces low-confidence ideas to the validate lane against `references/confidence-meter.md` (which survives); there is no second moment where the check would fire |\n| G10 | roadmap-shape:175 — step 12 \"Shape review.\" | **deleted** — review-of-a-roadmap is plan-review's When-to-use, and plan-review survives; the ceremony around it dies with the skill |\n| G11 | backlog-manage:103 — step 3 \"Identify session intent.\" | **deleted** — the gate disambiguated the multi-mode curation session; the modes die with the skill, and intake disambiguation is `shape:idea`'s When-to-use |\n| G12 | backlog-manage:123 — step 5 \"Confirm writes.\" | **absorbed** as a clause on G3: any route that mutates the idea bank or the tracker is announced and confirmed before the write |\n| G13 | design-doc:46 — gate 1 \"trigger check\" | `shape:design` Workflow — carried, extended into the typed branch point (doc vs technical spike vs product spike); the no-trigger → ADR referral is unchanged |\n| G14 | design-doc:54 — gate 3 \"problem section\" | `shape:design` doc branch — carried verbatim |\n| G15 | design-doc:74 — gate 8 \"operability plan\" | `shape:design` doc branch — carried verbatim |\n| G16 | backend-spike:80 — gate 1 \"Write the question.\" | `shape:design` spike branch — carried, shared across both spike variants |\n| G17 | backend-spike:105 — gate 4 \"Scope check.\" | `shape:design` spike branch — carried (throwaway scope + timebox discipline) |\n| G18 | backend-spike:114 — gate 6 \"Write the recommendation.\" | `shape:design` spike branch — exit gate, merged with G19 into one \"write the finding/recommendation\" gate over the merged spike record template |\n| G19 | product-spike:104 — gate 5 \"Write the finding.\" | merged into the same spike exit gate as G18; the semantics both sources share — a spike may not end without a written finding — are preserved once |\n\n**The delivery-shape ledger (gates outside the fold but inside the risk).** The re-house must not inherit the marker count of an already-degraded baseline. Ledger for `shape:delivery`, from `45b49b9`: D1 committed-input (currently marked, line 82 — carried); D2 map-to-KRs (de-marked, substance at step 3); D3 Rule A1 design-first (de-marked, step 4); D4 five-section body (de-marked, step 6); D5 acceptance criterion on every task (de-marked, step-7 rules); D6 size check (de-marked, step-7 rules); D7 verify-plan (de-marked, step 9). Q4 decides re-mark vs recorded demotion; either way the N02 Done-when checks against **this ledger of seven**, not against \"count stays 1\".\n\nFor completeness, gates elsewhere (not at risk, listed so N08 has the full ledger): plan-review 6 (kept verbatim through N06's compression), render-html 3 (untouched), using-this-pack 0 (one prose mention of the convention, see NFR note), pr-prepare 2 + verify-implementation 2 (transfer with their skills).\n\n## Consequences\n\n**Positive.**\n\n- N02–N05 become mechanical compression jobs against one spec; their Done-whens check `wc -l`, gate headers vs this inventory, and `install.sh` resolution — no per-door judgement calls.\n- N08 gets its deletion/cleanup list from one place: 7 skill dirs, 7 wrappers, 2 orphaned references, the install.sh help-text block, the plugin-manifest description, and the `bin/eval-triggers` / `eval/` retargets.\n- Gate loss becomes a review-time diff, not a benchmark-time surprise — and the one silent loss that already happened (delivery-shape) is surfaced and tracked instead of inherited.\n- The naming rule is a convention (wrapper leaf = dir leaf = frontmatter name), not a lookup table.\n\n**Negative / costs.**\n\n- The door names are themselves a one-way door: once `using-this-pack`, README, and initiative C's prompts cite `/shape:idea` etc., a rename is a coordinated sweep. Accepted — that is exactly why this doc, not a door node, decides them, and why ADR 0005 records them at acceptance.\n- Dedicated curation and portfolio ceremony is gone; if a real planning-cycle need emerges, it returns as a new shaped initiative, not a resurrection of the deleted files (git history keeps them recoverable).\n- `shape:design` carries fold risk (568 → ≤350 hard) — mitigated by the kill-condition watch note on N05, with this doc's inventory as the non-negotiable floor: a fold that closes under 350 only by dropping a gate is a kill, not a ship.\n- `shape:delivery` is no longer the zero-risk skeleton N02 assumed: the contract-aware compression (Q1) adds real work to the walking-skeleton node. Accepted trade-off — the alternative (skeleton on a different door) couples plumbing risk to fold risk, which is worse.\n\n**Walking skeleton (Rule B2).** Required and already scheduled: **N02 (`shape:delivery` re-house) is the skeleton.** It exercises every seam this doc decides — directory move, wrapper retarget, symlink regeneration and prune, install.sh help-text edit, `bin/eval-triggers` retarget, router route, frontmatter cut — on the door with no *absorption* risk, before any content fold starts. N03–N05 build only after the skeleton proves the plumbing. (Q1 and Q4 record the scope corrections the skeleton inherits.)\n\n## Operability plan\n\nAdapted to a Markdown-only pack: \"production\" is every installed copy of the pack, and the fitness functions are deterministic shell checks.\n\n- **Metrics.** Per door node at Done-when: file line count; gate-header count vs this inventory (incl. the delivery ledger); `install.sh` re-run resolving the new verb; both `bin/` plan gates exiting 0 and `bin/eval-triggers` running (N02). Initiative-level: measured-set total (N06); benchmark pass rate (N07); legacy-citation grep count over the live surfaces (N08).\n- **Structured logs.** Each door PR body records the before/after line counts and the gate diff against the inventory table — the review trail KR1's drains grep for.\n- **Alerts (kill conditions, owner-routed).** Door >350 lines without dropping a gate → stop, surface to the owner, ship style reforms only (project brake). Benchmark <80% after two repair attempts (N07) → same brake. Both are written into the project description; nodes cite them.\n- **Rollback plan.** Each door + its source deletions land as one revertible unit. Steps: (1) `git revert` the door commit — restores the source skills and wrappers atomically; (2) re-run `install.sh` — regenerates wrappers and prunes the door's now-stale `shape-*` symlink (verified prune behaviour, `install.sh:77–84`); (3) verification gate: the old slash commands resolve and the door's verb is gone.\n- **Capacity headroom.** 1,831-line worst case (shape:design modelled at its flagged 350) vs the 2,200 cap → ≥369 lines (≈17%) headroom before the brake trips.\n- **Known failure modes.** (a) Silent gate loss in a fold → the inventory + delivery ledger are Done-when inputs, and N08 sweeps stragglers. (b) Fold overruns the cap → N05's watch note + kill condition. (c) Baseline drift between this doc and pickup (already happened once: delivery-shape 196→381 between issue-writing and pickup) → every door node re-measures its sources at pickup and records a baseline-refresh note when numbers moved. (d) Stale wrappers/symlinks/help-text after deletion → `install.sh` prune + the help-text edit per node + N08 grep. (e) Concurrent initiative-A churn in `skills/` → measured set is pinned by filename, immune to additions. (f) Compression deletes normative contract from shape:delivery → protected-content list in Constraints + the `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` overflow route + `bin/check-plan-framing` re-run.\n- **Upstream dependencies.** `install.sh` wrapper/symlink generation and help text; `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (the frontmatter/body spec of record); ADR 0004 (verbs); `bin/eval-triggers` + `eval/` fixtures. A change to any of these reopens this doc by ADR.\n- **Downstream dependencies.** N02–N06 Done-whens; N08's sweep list; initiative C's supervisor prompts (will cite door verbs literally — they bind after this doc is accepted, which is what makes the naming a one-way door); `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` and the example plans (path citations updated in N08); `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` (description rewritten in N06).\n\n## Open questions\n\n| Q | Owner | Resolution gate |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Q1.** shape:delivery sizing: the 381 lines include deliberate, recent contract content (196 at `cfe42f6` → 381 via `ac931a7`/`372f09f`), so ≤300 cannot be reached by a density pass alone. This doc's recommendation: contract-aware compression with `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` as the overflow home for protected detail (the file already exists and is the contract's natural record). Confirm, or raise shape:delivery's cap to \"≤350 flagged\" instead? N02's first task becomes classifying the `cfe42f6..HEAD` diff into protected vs compressible lines. | Anton, at doc acceptance | N02's at-pickup task breakdown — blocked until answered |\n| **Q2.** Orphaned references: delete `references/portfolio-themes.md` and `references/dora-metrics.md` once roadmap-shape dies? (Default per this doc: delete both; dora-metrics is already uncited by any skill today.) | N08 pickup | N08's sweep task Done-when |\n| **Q3.** Confirm the measured set: KR1's baseline counted the pre-initiative-A 14-skill set; this doc pins the measured set to the seven survivor files above, excluding the transferred pr-prepare and verify-implementation. Per N01's to-verify assumption, the owner confirms at acceptance rather than the KR being silently rewritten. | Anton, at doc acceptance | this doc's acceptance — N06's footprint check binds to the answer |\n| **Q4.** delivery-shape's six de-marked gates (ledger D2–D7): re-mark them as `### N. Gate:` headers at the N02 re-house (this doc's recommendation — the substance is present and the demotion looks like rewrite collateral, not intent), or record the demotion as deliberate? | Anton, at doc acceptance | N02's at-pickup task breakdown — the gate-count fitness for shape:delivery binds to the answer |\n\n## Review record\n\nReviewed through the `plan-review` exit gate (Full tier, fresh-context adversarial pass + structured buckets): REVISE → conditions cleared in this revision. Record: `docs/plan-reviews/front-door-ia/review.md`. Acceptance (status → accepted, ADR 0005 filed, Q1/Q3/Q4 answered) is the owner's call.\n"
}

Prefer a clear story over a list of disconnected facts.\nProject Goals and OKRs\nExplain the gap between the current state, the desired state, and the cost of staying where you are.\nUse Situation, Complication, Question, Answer logic internally, but do not use those labels as headings.\nMake success measurable.\nBad:\n\"Improve the efficiency of our API and utilize better caching.\"\nGood:\n\"Our API serves 1k RPS today. The Q3 launch requires 10k RPS, which will exceed current capacity. We will re-architect the load balancer to support 15k RPS.\"\nBad:\n\"Significantly improve dashboard speed.\"\nGood:\n\"Reduce P95 dashboard load time from 4.5s to under 800ms using Edge Workers.\"\nDesign Docs\nProblem: Define the current state and the cost of the status quo.\nContext: Anchor the reader in stable facts before introducing the change or conflict.\nConstraints: Group non-functional requirements by structure or impact.\nAlternatives: Compare distinct options and state the practical effect of each one.\nRecommended approach: Start with what the team already knows, then introduce the proposed change.\nOperability plan: Define verifiable rollback gates, monitoring signals, and ownership.\nMilestones and Deliverables\nUse milestones as summary achievements, not labels such as \"Phase 1\" or \"Research.\"\nGroup work into non-overlapping containers.\nOrder groups by sequence, system structure, or impact.\nBad:\n\"Frontend; Data; Implementation.\"\nGood:\n\"Built responsive React components; Migrated user records to a sharded Postgres cluster.\"\nBad:\n\"Security; Authentication; Login Page.\"\nGood:\n\"Identity management; Data encryption at rest.\"\nTasks, Stories, Bugs, and Refactors\nEvery task needs a concrete \"done when\" state.\nAvoid blank tasks such as \"Investigate latency\" unless the output is named.\nUse active voice.\nBad:\n\"Perform an investigation into the latency situation.\"\nGood:\n\"Done when: The report identifies the three highest-latency queries in the /orders endpoint.\"\nBad:\n\"As a user, I want the utilization of an automated system.\"\nGood:\n\"As a user, I want to reset my password through an email link so I can regain access in under 2 minutes.\"\nVocabulary\nUse exact technical terms when they are accurate. Replace metaphors only when they hide the cause.\nPrefer \"essential\" or \"required\" over vague intensifiers such as \"vital.\"\nReplace \"bottleneck\" with the specific shortage, delay, queue, lock, or capacity limit when known.\nPrefer \"plan\" or \"spec\" over \"blueprint\" unless discussing an actual final design.\nPrefer \"limit\" or \"maximum\" over \"ceiling.\"\nPrefer \"use\" over \"utilization.\"\nPrefer \"implement\" over \"implementation\" when describing action.\nPrefer \"communicate with,\" \"connect to,\" or \"call\" over \"interface with.\"\nPrefer \"affect\" over \"impact\" as a verb.\nDelete \"basically,\" \"actually,\" \"quite,\" \"it should be noted that,\" and similar throat-clearing.\nReplace \"at this point in time\" with \"now.\"\nAgent Review Checklist\nBefore returning an artifact, check:\nDoes it move from problem to solution in a clear order?\nDo subtasks explain how to achieve the milestone or why the milestone matters?\nDid pruning preserve assumptions, risks, dependencies, constraints, and success criteria?\nIs every task's done state visible and verifiable?\nDo important terms or conclusions appear where readers will notice them?\nDoes it sound like a capable colleague wrote it?",

12	
13	# Front-door IA — absorption map, survivor manifest, naming schema, gate inventory
14	
15	**Trigger (Rule A1).** Three triggers hold; any one suffices: the deliverable breaks into six nodes (N01–N06, > ~5); the absorption map is a **one-way door** (folded skills are deleted, and the door namespace publishes into every install through `install.sh`'s wrapper generation and `shape-*` symlinks); and it touches **shared infrastructure** (command wrappers, skill symlinks, `using-this-pack` routing, the plugin manifest).
16	
17	**Scope boundary.** This doc fixes maps, manifests, and naming rules only. Door prose is N02–N05's work at pickup. Any sentence here that drafts a door's actual wording is scope creep — reject it at review.
18	
19	## Problem
20	
21	The shaping half of the pack is spread across twelve skills (2,599 lines at the 2026-06-12 baseline) plus two execution-side survivors, with overlapping When-to-use sections that force a routing guess on every session — `using-this-pack` papers over the ambiguity rather than removing it. Three skills sit over the pack's own 300-line cap (`delivery-shape` 381, `plan-review` 369, `roadmap-shape` 303), so the pack fails its own authoring standard.
22	
23	Four door builds (N02–N05) and a utility pass (N06) are queued to consolidate this, but they are blocked on decisions no single node can safely make alone: which sections of eight legacy skills fold into which door, what is deliberately deleted, what the surviving file set is, what everything is named, and where every `[GATE]` lands. Decided per-door, those four judgement calls drift apart, and a dropped gate surfaces only at the benchmark (N07) or in a live shaping run — not at review. The risk is not hypothetical: the source library already contains one silent gate loss (delivery-shape — see Context), which this doc converts from an undetected drift into a tracked decision.
24	
25	Affected parties: the door authors (N02–N06 wait on this doc for their task breakdowns), the N08 reference sweep (needs one deletion list), drain-cycle workers and Anton driving interactively (every session pays the routing and context cost), and initiative C (its supervisor prompts will cite door verbs literally).
26	
27	Current behaviour: no absorption map, no pinned measured set, no naming decision, no gate inventory. Desired behaviour: one accepted IA document that fixes all four, so the door builds become mechanical compression jobs against a stable spec.
28	
29	## Context
30	
31	- **Confirmed intent (not re-litigated here).** `docs/ideas/lifecycle-expansion.md:40` records the owner-confirmed pruning: roadmap-shape, backlog-manage, app-calibrate deleted; idea-triage folds into `shape:idea`; spikes fold into `shape:design`; plan-review survives as the design/delivery exit gate; render-html survives as a utility. This doc inherits those calls and decides the *section-level* mechanics.
32	- **Namespace inherited from A/N04.** ADR 0004 reserves both halves of the verb namespace (`shape:*` / `exec:*`) in one table and explicitly leaves the shaping-half consolidation to this initiative. This doc consumes that table; it does not reopen it. The shaping **leaf** names decided here get their own decision record: **ADR 0005, filed at acceptance**, mirroring the ADR 0004 pattern.
33	- **Prior IA superseded.** `docs/designs/shaping-pipeline.md` (2026-06-03, status Proposed, never accepted) is the previous shaping IA. Its surviving decisions already live in delivery-shape (five-section ticket body, size gate); its `execution-breakdown` construct was deleted in `2c03385`. This doc supersedes it; N08 need not rewrite it — superseded docs are historical record, like CHANGELOG.
34	- **Anatomy already reformed.** `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (revised in `e0cd32f`, 2026-06-12) already specifies the two-field frontmatter and the eleven-section body. The frontmatter-cut spec below binds the doors to it and lists which survivors still carry legacy fields.
35	- **Baseline drift since the issues were written — delivery-shape, measured against git.** The cycle issues carry 2026-06-10 numbers. Re-measured 2026-06-12 (`wc -l skills/*/SKILL.md`): the anatomy-prune (`cfe42f6`) cut design-doc 243→174 and initiative-shape to 175 (under cap, six-field shape intact). delivery-shape was **196 lines at `cfe42f6`** and was then deliberately grown to **381** (`ac931a7` — walking-skeleton conditionality; `372f09f`/`fc9b497` — +293/−108 overall). The added content is **normative contract, not padding**: the `Done when:` discipline, the size check, dependency ordering, and the model-routing rubric that `bin/check-plan-framing` enforces. N02's premise ("no fold needed, already under cap at 266") is doubly stale — see Q1.

The current file carries **one** marked gate (step 2). The substance of the other six survives as unmarked steps/prose (steps 3, 4, 6, 9 and the step-7 task rules), but a fitness check that counts gate markers would bless their deletion. See Q4 and the inventory's delivery-shape ledger.
37	- **Initiative A is concurrently adding execution skills** to `skills/` (build, debugging, exec-pickup, execution-review, pr-finishing, simplify), so `wc -l skills/*/SKILL.md` drifts for reasons outside this initiative. The measured set below pins exactly which files KR1 counts.

39	
40	## Constraints
41	
42	**Functional.**
43	
44	- Every gate in the eight folded skills has a destination section or a written deletion rationale (the issue's mandatory Done condition).
45	- The six-field initiative shape emitted by today's initiative-shape is byte-stable across the fold — `shape:delivery`'s gate 2 consumes it verbatim.
46	- `bin/walk-delivery-plan` and `bin/check-plan-framing` keep exiting 0 on existing example plans after the delivery re-house.
47	- `bin/eval-triggers` keeps running: its `CANDIDATE_SKILLS` array hardcodes `delivery-shape` and `initiative-shape` (`bin/eval-triggers:32–35`) and reads `eval/delivery-shape-triggers.md`. N02 retargets the delivery entry; N04 retargets the initiative entry. The eval files themselves are renamed or re-pointed in the same change.
48	- delivery-shape's **protected content** survives the re-house and compression: the step-7 contract rules (`Done when:`, size check, dependency ordering, model routing) and the six de-marked gates' substance. Extraction to the existing `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` is the sanctioned overflow route; deletion is not.
49	- Folded source directories and their wrappers are deleted in the same change as the door that absorbs them (revert restores both sides atomically).
50	- No stack-prescriptive content enters door prose (repo rule); the doors stay stack-agnostic.

55	|---|---|---|

Known false positive: `using-this-pack:60` explains the `[GATE]` convention in prose — N06's rewrite rephrases it or the sweep whitelists it |
59	| Benchmark stability | plan-review benchmark ≥90% after reform (kill condition: <80% after two repair attempts) | N07 rerun of `skills/plan-review/evals/evals.json` (kept byte-identical by N06) |

Exempt as historical record: CHANGELOG, `examples/delivery-plans/`, `docs/plan-reviews/`, superseded docs | N08's sweep task |
61	| Wrapper collision | No two commands share a leaf name at the slash surface | diff over `.claude/commands/` after authoring (same fitness as A/N04's NFR) |
62	
63	## Alternatives considered
64	
65	The fold/delete/survive calls are inherited (Context); the open decision this doc owns is the **naming schema** — directory names, wrapper names, and how they bind to the `shape:*` verbs.
66	
67	**Alt 1 — bare phase-leaf directories (`skills/idea/`, `skills/project/`, `skills/design/`, `skills/delivery/`), wrapper leaf = directory leaf.**
68	*Description.* Directory, frontmatter `name`, and wrapper file all use the phase leaf; `install.sh` derives the installed symlink `shape-<leaf>` and the wrapper publishes `/shape:<leaf>`. Exactly the rule the pack already applies (`delivery.md` → `/shape:delivery`).
69	*Blast radius if wrong.* Low. Leafs are generic out of context ("design") but never appear out of context — the installed surface always carries the `shape-`/`shape:` prefix.
70	*Reversal cost.* Low — `install.sh` already prunes stale symlinks on re-install; a rename is a directory move plus one wrapper edit.
71	
72	**Alt 2 — suffixed directories (`skills/idea-shape/`, …).**
73	*Description.* Keep a descriptive suffix on every directory.
74	*Blast radius if wrong.* Installed names stutter (`shape-idea-shape`); wrapper leaf no longer equals directory leaf, so the naming rule needs a lookup table instead of a convention.
75	*Reversal cost.* Same mechanics as Alt 1 but with a worse steady state; rejected on stutter and rule complexity.
76	
77	**Alt 3 — keep current directories, retarget wrappers only.**
78	*Description.* `/shape:idea` points at `skills/idea-triage/`, etc.; no directory moves.
79	*Blast radius if wrong.* Permanent drift between verb and directory; the KR's cleanup grep ("zero legacy citations") can never pass because the legacy names *are* the layout; the router has to explain the mismatch forever.
80	*Reversal cost.* High in practice — the drift compounds with every doc that cites a path.
81	
82	**Alt 4 — do nothing (no consolidation).**
83	*Description.* Keep the twelve shaping skills.
84	*Blast radius if wrong.* KR1 fails by definition; the routing ambiguity and over-cap skills persist.
85	*Reversal cost.* n/a — this is the failure mode the initiative exists to remove.
86	
87	## Recommended approach
88	
89	**Adopt Alt 1.** The four artefacts below are the spec the door nodes build against.
90	
91	### 1. Naming schema (consumes ADR 0004's table; recorded in ADR 0005 at acceptance)
92	
93	| Verb | Directory | Frontmatter `name` | Wrapper (repo) | Slash command | Installed symlink |
94	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
95	| `shape:idea` | `skills/idea/` | `idea` | `.claude/commands/idea.md` | `/shape:idea` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-idea` |
96	| `shape:project` | `skills/project/` | `project` | `.claude/commands/project.md` | `/shape:project` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-project` |
97	| `shape:design` | `skills/design/` | `design` | `.claude/commands/design.md` | `/shape:design` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-design` |
98	| `shape:delivery` | `skills/delivery/` | `delivery` | `.claude/commands/delivery.md` (retargeted) | `/shape:delivery` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-delivery` |
99	| `shape:plan-review` | `skills/plan-review/` (unchanged) | `plan-review` | `.claude/commands/plan-review.md` (unchanged) | `/shape:plan-review` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-plan-review` |
100	| `shape:render-html` | `skills/render-html/` (unchanged) | `render-html` | `.claude/commands/render-html.md` (unchanged) | `/shape:render-html` | `~/.claude/skills/shape-render-html` |
101	| — (router, model-invoked) | `skills/using-this-pack/` (unchanged) | `using-this-pack` | none (status quo) | — | `~/.claude/skills/shape-using-this-pack` |

103	Rules the table encodes: wrapper leaf = directory leaf = frontmatter `name`; `install.sh` derives the symlink (`shape-<leaf>`) and prunes stale links on re-install (verified `install.sh:77–84`). **Not derived — owned by hand:** the help text at `install.sh:161–173` hardcodes the command list and currently prints eight legacy verbs; each door node updates its own lines there (N02 proves the seam), and N08's grep catches stragglers.

Created: `idea.md`, `project.md`, `design.md`. Retargeted in place: `delivery.md`. Out of this initiative's scope: `exec/*`, `stop-the-line.md`, `ta[REDACTED_SK].md`, `pr-prepare.md`, `verify-implementation.md` (execution half — initiative A owns their fate; N08 only sweeps citations).

131	### 3. Frontmatter-cut spec
132	
133	Per `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (already revised): exactly two fields survive — `name`, `description` (what the skill does, then "Use when …", then trigger phrases). The eight legacy fields die: `pack`, `lifecycle_stage`, `principles_implemented`, `length_target`, `author`, `predecessor`, `kept_from_predecessor`, `changed_from_predecessor`. Taxonomy they carried lives in README tables and git history.
134	
135	State of play: initiative-shape, design-doc, delivery-shape are already cut. Still carrying legacy fields among survivors: **plan-review (10 fields), render-html (10), using-this-pack (6)** — N06 applies the cut. The folded sources' frontmatter dies with their files.
136	
137	### 4. Absorption map — all 14 baseline skills
138	
139	Disposition vocabulary: **fold** (content moves into a door section), **absorb** (a clause or fragment moves; the section dies), **delete** (dies with written rationale), **survive** (file continues, possibly re-housed), **transfer** (leaves the shaping half for initiative A's execution half).
140	
141	A door's body follows the eleven-section anatomy (Title → Purpose → When to use → Do not use when → Inputs → Outputs → Workflow → Artefact template → Red flags → Exit criteria → Related). Two standing rules applied throughout, recorded once instead of per-row:
142	
143	- **"Common rationalisations" sections** (idea-triage, app-calibrate, roadmap-shape, backlog-manage, backend-spike, product-spike, plan-review, render-html) have no slot in the anatomy. Rule: load-bearing rows merge into the door's **Red flags**; the rest is explanatory redundancy and is deleted. plan-review and render-html keep theirs until their own density passes apply the same rule.
144	- **"References" sections** become the door's **Related** section, citing only references the door actually uses.
145

197	| Workflow (gates at steps 1, 4, 6) | fold → spike-branch steps; gates carried (inventory G16–G18); steps shared with the product variant where identical (write-the-question, scope/timebox) |
198	| Recommendation template (Question / Failure example / Options considered / Scope / Recommended approach / Confidence and semantics impact / Follow-up) | fold → merged **spike record** template, technical-variant fields |
199	| Rationalisations / Red flags / Verification / References | per standing rules; confidence-meter stays cited |
200	
201	**product-spike (189) → fold into `shape:design`** as the product-spike variant. Same pattern: When-to-use triggers fold into the spike branch; the finding template (Question / Approach / Observations / Finding / Recommendation / Prototype artefact) merges into the spike record template as the product-variant fields; gate at step 5 merges into the shared spike exit gate (inventory G19). The throwaway-artefact discipline (prototype is disposable, finding is the deliverable) survives as spike-branch prose.
202	
203	**delivery-shape (381) → survive, re-housed as `shape:delivery`** (N02, the walking skeleton). Every section folds 1:1 into `skills/delivery/`; the emitted plan format and `bin/` gate compatibility are asserted by N02's Done-when, and the `bin/eval-triggers` entry is retargeted. Reaching ≤300 is **not a density pass** — the growth from 196 (`cfe42f6`) to 381 is recent, deliberate contract content. The sanctioned route is **contract-aware compression**: protected content (Constraints) stays in the skill or moves intact to `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` with a citation; nothing protected is deleted. Gate handling per Q4: the six de-marked gates are re-marked (or their demotion is owner-recorded) before any compression starts, so the gate fitness check has a true baseline.
204	
205	**plan-review (369) → survive** as a utility. N06 compresses to ≤300 as pure density reform: all six `[GATE]` steps verbatim, `evals/evals.json` byte-identical, tier-selection and B1–B8 axes unchanged. Frontmatter cut applies.
206	
207	**render-html (131) → survive** as a utility, unchanged except the frontmatter cut. Its references are **bundled skill-local files** (`skills/render-html/references/html-patterns.md`, `html-skeleton.md`) cited via `<skill-base>/references/…` — they exist, travel with the directory, and stay outside the measured set.
208	
209	**using-this-pack (119) → survive** as the router, rewritten by N06 to route exactly the four doors plus utilities (and to point execution work at `exec:pickup`), bounded at ≤150 lines. Frontmatter cut applies (currently 6 fields). No gates; meta-pack anatomy exemptions apply. Line 60's prose explanation of the `[GATE]` convention is rephrased or whitelisted so N08's straggler grep stays clean.
210	
211	**pr-prepare (163) and verify-implementation (123) → transfer** to the execution half. ADR 0004 reserves their successors (`exec:finish`, `exec:verify`); initiative A owns the skills, their gates (2 + 2), and their wrappers. They leave the shaping measured set. N08 sweeps shaping-side citations only.
212	
213	**Reference disposition** (consequence of the map): survive — app-context-schema, confidence-meter, ice-scoring, kano-classification, initiative-types, kr-quality-templates, task-sizing, nfr-categories, plus render-html's bundled pair. Orphaned → N08 deletion list — portfolio-themes (only roadmap-shape cites it), dora-metrics (cited by no skill; README/CHANGELOG only).
214

244	
245	## Consequences
246	
247	**Positive.**
248	
249	- N02–N05 become mechanical compression jobs against one spec; their Done-whens check `wc -l`, gate headers vs this inventory, and `install.sh` resolution — no per-door judgement calls.
250	- N08 gets its deletion/cleanup list from one place: 7 skill dirs, 7 wrappers, 2 orphaned references, the install.sh help-text block, the plugin-manifest description, and the `bin/eval-triggers` / `eval/` retargets.
251	- Gate loss becomes a review-time diff, not a benchmark-time surprise — and the one silent loss that already happened (delivery-shape) is surfaced and tracked instead of inherited.
252	- The naming rule is a convention (wrapper leaf = dir leaf = frontmatter name), not a lookup table.
253	
254	**Negative / costs.**
255	
256	- The door names are themselves a one-way door: once `using-this-pack`, README, and initiative C's prompts cite `/shape:idea` etc., a rename is a coordinated sweep. Accepted — that is exactly why this doc, not a door node, decides them, and why ADR 0005 records them at acceptance.
257	- Dedicated curation and portfolio ceremony is gone; if a real planning-cycle need emerges, it returns as a new shaped initiative, not a resurrection of the deleted files (git history keeps them recoverable).
258	- `shape:design` carries fold risk (568 → ≤350 hard) — mitigated by the kill-condition watch note on N05, with this doc's inventory as the non-negotiable floor: a fold that closes under 350 only by dropping a gate is a kill, not a ship.
259	- `shape:delivery` is no longer the zero-risk skeleton N02 assumed: the contract-aware compression (Q1) adds real work to the walking-skeleton node. Accepted trade-off — the alternative (skeleton on a different door) couples plumbing risk to fold risk, which is worse.
260	
261	**Walking skeleton (Rule B2).** Required and already scheduled: **N02 (`shape:delivery` re-house) is the skeleton.** It exercises every seam this doc decides — directory move, wrapper retarget, symlink regeneration and prune, install.sh help-text edit, `bin/eval-triggers` retarget, router route, frontmatter cut — on the door with no *absorption* risk, before any content fold starts. N03–N05 build only after the skeleton proves the plumbing. (Q1 and Q4 record the scope corrections the skeleton inherits.)
262	
263	## Operability plan
264	
265	Adapted to a Markdown-only pack: "production" is every installed copy of the pack, and the fitness functions are deterministic shell checks.
266	
267	- **Metrics.** Per door node at Done-when: file line count; gate-header count vs this inventory (incl. the delivery ledger); `install.sh` re-run resolving the new verb; both `bin/` plan gates exiting 0 and `bin/eval-triggers` running (N02). Initiative-level: measured-set total (N06); benchmark pass rate (N07); legacy-citation grep count over the live surfaces (N08).
268	- **Structured logs.** Each door PR body records the before/after line counts and the gate diff against the inventory table — the review trail KR1's drains grep for.
269	- **Alerts (kill conditions, owner-routed).** Door >350 lines without dropping a gate → stop, surface to the owner, ship style reforms only (project brake). Benchmark <80% after two repair attempts (N07) → same brake. Both are written into the project description; nodes cite them.
270	- **Rollback plan.** Each door + its source deletions land as one revertible unit. Steps: (1) `git revert` the door commit — restores the source skills and wrappers atomically; (2) re-run `install.sh` — regenerates wrappers and prunes the door's now-stale `shape-*` symlink (verified prune behaviour, `install.sh:77–84`); (3) verification gate: the old slash commands resolve and the door's verb is gone.
271	- **Capacity headroom.** 1,831-line worst case (shape:design modelled at its flagged 350) vs the 2,200 cap → ≥369 lines (≈17%) headroom before the brake trips.
272	- **Known failure modes.** (a) Silent gate loss in a fold → the inventory + delivery ledger are Done-when inputs, and N08 sweeps stragglers. (b) Fold overruns the cap → N05's watch note + kill condition. (c) Baseline drift between this doc and pickup (already happened once: delivery-shape 196→381 between issue-writing and pickup) → every door node re-measures its sources at pickup and records a baseline-refresh note when numbers moved. (d) Stale wrappers/symlinks/help-text after deletion → `install.sh` prune + the help-text edit per node + N08 grep. (e) Concurrent initiative-A churn in `skills/` → measured set is pinned by filename, immune to additions. (f) Compression deletes normative contract from shape:delivery → protected-content list in Constraints + the `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` overflow route + `bin/check-plan-framing` re-run.
273	- **Upstream dependencies.** `install.sh` wrapper/symlink generation and help text; `docs/skill-anatomy.md` (the frontmatter/body spec of record); ADR 0004 (verbs); `bin/eval-triggers` + `eval/` fixtures. A change to any of these reopens this doc by ADR.
274	- **Downstream dependencies.** N02–N06 Done-whens; N08's sweep list; initiative C's supervisor prompts (will cite door verbs literally — they bind after this doc is accepted, which is what makes the naming a one-way door); `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` and the example plans (path citations updated in N08); `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` (description rewritten in N06).
275	
276	## Open questions
277	
278	| Q | Owner | Resolution gate |
279	|---|---|---|
280	| **Q1.** shape:delivery sizing: the 381 lines include deliberate, recent contract content (196 at `cfe42f6` → 381 via `ac931a7`/`372f09f`), so ≤300 cannot be reached by a density pass alone. This doc's recommendation: contract-aware compression with `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md` as the overflow home for protected detail (the file already exists and is the contract's natural record). Confirm, or raise shape:delivery's cap to "≤350 flagged" instead? N02's first task becomes classifying the `cfe42f6..HEAD` diff into protected vs compressible lines. | Anton, at doc acceptance | N02's at-pickup task breakdown — blocked until answered |
281	| **Q2.** Orphaned references: delete `references/portfolio-themes.md` and `references/dora-metrics.md` once roadmap-shape dies? (Default per this doc: delete both; dora-metrics is already uncited by any skill today.) | N08 pickup | N08's sweep task Done-when |
282	| **Q3.** Confirm the measured set: KR1's baseline counted the pre-initiative-A 14-skill set; this doc pins the measured set to the seven survivor files above, excluding the transferred pr-prepare and verify-implementation. Per N01's to-verify assumption, the owner confirms at acceptance rather than the KR being silently rewritten. | Anton, at doc acceptance | this doc's acceptance — N06's footprint check binds to the answer |
283	| **Q4.** delivery-shape's six de-marked gates (ledger D2–D7): re-mark them as `### N. Gate:` headers at the N02 re-house (this doc's recommendation — the substance is present and the demotion looks like rewrite collateral, not intent), or record the demotion as deliberate? | Anton, at doc acceptance | N02's at-pickup task breakdown — the gate-count fitness for shape:delivery binds to the answer |
284	
285	## Review record
286	
287	Reviewed through the `plan-review` exit gate (Full tier, fresh-context adversarial pass + structured buckets): REVISE → conditions cleared in this revision. Record: `docs/plan-reviews/front-door-ia/review.md`. Acceptance (status → accepted, ADR 0005 filed, Q1/Q3/Q4 answered) is the owner's call.

# Rewrite `docs/designs/front-door-ia.md` per the writing guidelines (crit comment r_41ef9c)

## Context

Anton reviewed the IA design doc in crit and left one review-level comment: rewrite the doc using his writing guidelines — actor in the subject, action in the verb, concrete verbs over nominalizations, known-context-first sentence flow, SCQA logic in the Problem/Context sections, milestones as visible work products, vocabulary swaps (no "utilize"/"impact-as-verb"/throat-clearing), and the design-doc section guidance (Problem = current state + cost of status quo; Recommended approach = start from what's known, then the change; Operability = verifiable rollback gates, signals, ownership).

This is a **prose rewrite, not a content change**. The doc's normative payload — the four artefacts ABA-375 requires — must survive byte-for-byte in meaning: the naming schema table, the survivor manifest + measured-set command + 1,781/1,831/≥369 arithmetic, the frontmatter-cut spec, the 14-skill absorption map, the gate inventory G1–G19 + delivery ledger D1–D7, the NFR table, Q1–Q4 with owners and resolution gates, and the plan-review record reference. The plan-review APPROVE in `docs/plan-reviews/front-door-ia/review.md` stays valid only if no verdict-bearing fact changes.

## Changes (single file: `docs/designs/front-door-ia.md`)

1. **Problem** — restructure as: current state (12 shaping skills, 2,599 lines, 3 over their own 300-line cap, every session pays a routing guess) → cost of staying (four blocked door builds, per-door drift, gate loss that surfaces at benchmark time — the delivery-shape silent loss already happened) → desired state with the measurable target (one accepted IA; doors become mechanical jobs checked by `wc -l`, gate-header diff, install resolution; measured set ≤2,200). Convert "Affected parties: …" and "Current behaviour: … Desired behaviour: …" fragment lists into sentences with actors as subjects.
2. **Context** — keep the bullet structure (stable facts) but rewrite each bullet active-voice: "ADR 0004 reserves…", "The owner confirmed the pruning in lifecycle-expansion.md:40…", "Commit `ac931a7` grew delivery-shape from 196 to 381 lines of deliberate contract content…". Order each bullet known→new.
3. **Constraints** — keep both lists and the NFR table as-is structurally; de-nominalize the prose ("Extraction to … is the sanctioned overflow route" → "N02 may move protected detail intact to `docs/delivery-shape-contract.md`; it may not delete it").
4. **Alternatives** — keep the four alts; rewrite each *Description/Blast radius/Reversal cost* line so the actor acts ("`install.sh` derives the symlink…" is already right; fix passives like "Installed names stutter" framing where the cause hides).
5. **Recommended approach** — open from the known (the pack already applies wrapper-leaf = dir-leaf on `delivery.md`), then state the change. Tables 1–5 keep their exact content; surrounding prose gets the verb pass ("contract-aware compression" stays as a named term but the sentences around it use "compress", "fold", "delete", "move").
6. **Consequences / Operability / Open questions** — active-voice pass; Operability keeps every sub-field and the ordered rollback steps with per-step verification gates (already conforms; tighten wording). Q1–Q4 keep owners and gates verbatim, trim the run-on framing in Q1.
7. **Global vocabulary sweep** — kill throat-clearing ("The risk is not hypothetical", "for completeness"-style framing where it adds nothing), replace nominalizations where a verb fits, keep defined terms that are load-bearing vocabulary (fold/absorb/delete/survive/transfer; "contract-aware compression"; "measured set"; "walking skeleton").
8. **Do not touch**: frontmatter fields (update `last_updated` only if the date changes — it's already 2026-06-12), table contents, line numbers, commit hashes, gate IDs, the arithmetic, the `wc -l` command block.
